## Review: AI agent Phase 2 — round 3

**Final verdict: CHANGES REQUIRED.** Reviewer: Codex. Date: 2026-10-13. Branch: `feat/ai-agent`; baseline: HEAD plus untracked Phase 2 files. **2 P1; no P0, P2 or nits.** Phase 1 verdict unchanged; Phase 3 not approved.

Read `AGENTS.md`, `/Users/lamtung/.codex/RTK.md`, `.agent/WORKFLOW.md`, both prior Phase 2 reviews and `.agent/plans/ai-agent.md`, including round-2/round-3 fixes. Reviewed tracked `git diff HEAD`, untracked catalog executors, `sql_safety.rs`, `agent*.ts` and component/hook tests; traced actual Run and persistence paths. Ignored `.claude/launch.json`. Only this report edited; no source edits, staging or commit.

### Findings

1. **P1 — PostgreSQL identity gate permits custom casts/operators that call unsafe built-ins.** `src-tauri/src/ai/agent/catalog/postgres.rs:111–149`; `src-tauri/src/ai/agent/sql_safety.rs:487–550`. The gate rejects user-schema functions, policies and domain constraints, then checks view SQL. It never checks `pg_cast` or `pg_operator`; absence of user functions does not imply those objects are pure. In an otherwise clean database, an administrator can install `CREATE CAST (bigint AS boolean) WITH FUNCTION pg_catalog.pg_try_advisory_lock(bigint);`. This adds no user function, policy, domain constraint or view, so `only_builtin_code` still returns true. Suggested `SELECT CAST(CAST(424242 AS bigint) AS boolean)` passes the exact Rust classifier and frontend heuristic, acquires a session advisory lock through the cast, and gets `read_only`/Run. A custom prefix operator is another route: `CREATE OPERATOR public.! (RIGHTARG = bigint, FUNCTION = pg_catalog.pg_try_advisory_lock);`, followed by `SELECT ! CAST(424242 AS bigint)` when public is on the search path. Both query classifications were independently reproduced with current code. Server execution is inferred from documented cast/operator dispatch and advisory-lock semantics, not claimed as a live reproduction. **Fix:** establish purity of resolved cast/operator implementations and other indirect execution paths, or conservatively reject custom dispatch objects/unsupported syntax. Add live adversarial fixtures through backend safety and frontend eligibility; preserve normal casts and reporting only where verified safe. References: [CREATE CAST](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/sql-createcast.html), [CREATE OPERATOR](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/17/sql-createoperator.html), [advisory-lock functions](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/current/functions-admin.html#FUNCTIONS-ADVISORY-LOCKS).

2. **P1 — Run trusts historical connection safety without checking the execution-time catalog.** `src/lib/ai-assistant/agent.ts:186–237`; `src/components/ai-assistant/AiAssistantMessageParts.tsx:148`; `src/components/ai-assistant/AiAssistantMessageCard.tsx:133–150`. The new connection-bound check runs only when building the answer. Its `read_only` label is persisted in message parts; Run checks only legacy `isReadOnlySql` and executes through unrestricted `runSqlQuery`. Concrete sequence: receive a preview for `SELECT lower(1)` on a clean PostgreSQL database; later create the round-2 `public.lower(integer)` overload that calls `pg_advisory_lock`; click the existing Run button (or reload the saved chat and click it). The current gate would return unknown if called again, but no call occurs, and execution resolves the side-effecting overload. This requires no forged IPC or model safety field. Authentication does not validate SQL purity. **Fix:** treat persisted preview safety as display metadata; revalidate the exact SQL against the current runtime connection immediately before Run, after any authentication wait. Prefer one backend validate-and-execute boundary with a defined connection/catalog consistency contract rather than two independent pool operations. Fail closed while checking; retain Insert. Add mounted-card regressions for catalog changes, saved-chat reload and a changed runtime connection. This is static path verification; no live desktop lock acquisition claimed.

### Disposition of the five round-2 findings

| Finding                                | Round-3 disposition                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| MariaDB executable comments            | **Fixed.** Tokenizer rejects `/*M!`, `/*m!`, versioned forms, `/*!` and `/*+` before stripping comments. Rust regression covers original hidden GET_LOCK. Backend unknown prevents frontend `read_only` even though legacy heuristic remains permissive.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Connection-bound built-in identities   | **Partially fixed; P1s above remain.** Typed command receives runtime UUID; syntax and `only_builtin_code` must both succeed; errors/oversize/unsupported connections fail closed. PostgreSQL rejects the original user overload, RLS/domain constraints and unsafe views. MySQL/MariaDB reject visible stored functions and missing/unsafe view definitions. SQLite returns true based on the app's lack of registered UDFs/extensions; inspected drivers confirm that premise. These are catalog-wide conservative checks, not per-query resolved identity proofs.            |
| SQLite disarm-before-release           | **Fixed.** `Held::drop` clears handle/marks finished under arm mutex before dropping connection guard, including panic. Pre-arm exits retain `Finish`. Worker rechecks cancellation/deadline under arm mutex. Normal/panic reuse regression passes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Legacy metadata bounds/idempotent fit  | **Fixed for production scope/envelope inputs.** Loaded ids, titles, provider/target/language fields are bounded; invalid roles dropped. Residual oversized session metadata becomes one bounded empty session. Metadata-only idempotence and mounted-hook bounded-render tests pass; authoritative visible state still receives fitting before storage. Arbitrarily oversized caller-supplied scope keys or an impossibly small custom `maxBytes` are outside those tested production inputs; do not interpret the helper's “always fits” comment as a universal API guarantee. |
| Cleanup budget / Done / slot ownership | **Fixed in inspected production paths.** Interrupt + unwind + disconnect budgets total 3.5 s, compile-time asserted below 5 s. Coordinator keeps waiting after grace expiry; Done/return occur after tracker drains and command then drops guard. Slow-cleanup regression passes. No production lifecycle deadlock found; limitations below apply.                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

### Lifecycle and regression assessment

- PostgreSQL/MySQL pool acquisition is cancellation-aware and bounded. Network work, transaction setup/rollback, interrupt transport and interrupted MySQL disconnect are inside timeouts. Owned tasks retain targets through cleanup; interrupted PostgreSQL clients are removed from pool. The coordinator's new wait does not hold a resource needed by those tasks to finish.
- SQLite worker lock acquisition checks cancel/deadline repeatedly; watcher lifetime is bounded and shares arm state with worker. Fixed metadata statements remain main-only. `Held` fixes late editor interruption. The tracker wait itself is **unbounded**: its compile-time budget assertion covers timeout constants, not blocking-worker scheduling, synchronous row decoding or every SQLite/native operation. No concrete permanently stuck production path was reproduced; this remains a limitation requiring native slow-operation/runtime-stall testing. Do not abort a tracker wait and release the slot while work remains alive. Current beyond-grace test uses a cooperative fake, not slow native interrupt/disconnect plus registry capacity.
- Catalog tools remain fixed, parameter-bound metadata statements; no model SQL or application-row tool registered. Column-details opt-out strips defaults/comments before serialization. Step summaries contain fixed tool names/status/count information, not raw arguments/results/provider replay. No new raw SQL/data logging found.
- History/context forwarding, legacy fallback before tool execution, no post-tool fallback, semantic truncation propagation, visible eviction and summary-only persistence retain their round-2 fixes. Run remains the safety gap above; `runSqlQuery` is not the fixed catalog executor despite wording in Claude's identity fix notes.

### Independent verification and limitations

- `npm run build`: **passed**, existing externalization/dynamic-import/chunk-size/plugin-timing warnings.
- `npm run test:run`: **122 files / 685 tests passed**.
- `cargo test --offline --locked ai::agent -- --skip http_`: **80 passed / 7 ignored / 207 filtered out** (two suites).
- Full `cargo test --offline --locked`, default and app-store: **274 passed / 12 failed / 8 ignored each**. All failures are socket-bind `PermissionDenied: Operation not permitted` in agent HTTP, legacy chat and transport fixtures: **environment limits, not findings**.
- `cargo clippy --offline --locked --all-targets`, both flavors: **passed**, 6/7 existing warnings outside agent code. `cargo fmt --all -- --check` and `git diff --check`: passed.
- Temporary `/tmp` Rust harness used the exact classifier with serde-only annotations removed; both cast/operator examples returned ReadOnly. Node harness extracted the exact frontend heuristic; both returned true. Source was not modified for these reproductions.
- Ignored live identity/cancellation fixtures inspected, not independently executed. Claude's reported PostgreSQL/MySQL/MariaDB container runs are implementer evidence. No independent live DB/provider desktop round trip, native UI/MAS lease check, changed-catalog Run smoke or stalled-native-worker test. Existing green tests do not cover the two P1 paths.

Next: Claude fixes identity dispatch and execution-time Run validation, adds targeted regressions, records evidence and requests re-review.

## Review: AI agent Phase 2 — round 4

**Final verdict: CHANGES REQUIRED.** Reviewer: Codex. Date: 2026-10-13. Branch: `feat/ai-agent`; baseline: `git diff HEAD` plus untracked Phase 2 files. **1 P1; no P0, P2 or nits.** Both round-3 P1 findings are fixed in code. Phase 1 verdict unchanged; Phase 3 not approved.

Read `AGENTS.md`, `/Users/lamtung/.codex/RTK.md`, `.agent/WORKFLOW.md`, all three prior Phase 2 reviews and Claude's “Phase 2 — Review fixes, round 4” in `.agent/plans/ai-agent.md`. Reviewed safety/catalog/coordinator changes, frontend agent routing, preview execution, persistence and regression tests. Ignored `.claude/launch.json`. Only this review report edited; no source changes, staging or commit.

### Findings

1. **P1 — MySQL/MariaDB trust writable system-schema views without inspecting their code.** `src-tauri/src/ai/agent/catalog/mysql.rs:165–195`, especially `:182` and `SYSTEM_SCHEMAS` at `:25`. `only_builtin_code` excludes every view in `mysql` and `sys`, but neither the SQL classifier nor Run restricts table references to the catalog tool's advertised user schemas. In a database with no other disqualifying objects, an administrator can create `CREATE VIEW mysql.agent_lock_view AS SELECT GET_LOCK('agent_round4_lock', 0) AS acquired;` and grant the connected user SELECT on it. Suggested `SELECT acquired FROM mysql.agent_lock_view` passes both syntax checks; the gate skips the only unsafe view, returns true, and both answer-time classification and the new execution-time re-check enable unrestricted `runSqlQuery`. Selecting the view acquires a session advisory lock. This needs no DDL race, stale label, forged IPC or new stored function. The existing live fixture already tests the same GET_LOCK view in a user schema, but not an excluded schema. **Fix:** do not treat schema names as proof of immutable/pure view or routine identities. Inspect all reachable definitions (fail closed for missing definitions), or enforce a query/reference boundary that excludes unverified objects and their transitive dependencies. Preserve safe ordinary reporting where that can be established. Add `mysql`/`sys` unsafe-view fixtures through backend safety and mounted Run eligibility for both engines.

   **Evidence:** temporary `/tmp` harness compiled the exact current Rust classifier with serialization-only annotations removed: `SELECT acquired FROM mysql.agent_lock_view` returned `ReadOnly` for both `mysql` and `mariadb`; an extracted exact frontend `isReadOnlySql` returned `true`. Catalog exclusion and Run routing verified statically. Server execution is inferred from documented view evaluation and GET_LOCK behavior, **not independently run**: Docker socket access returned permission denied. Creating the fixture requires CREATE VIEW privilege in that schema; clicking Run requires only the granted SELECT privilege. Official references: [MySQL CREATE VIEW](https://dev.mysql.com/doc/refman/8.4/en/create-view.html), [MariaDB CREATE VIEW](https://mariadb.com/docs/server/server-usage/views/create-view), [MariaDB GET_LOCK](https://mariadb.com/docs/server/reference/sql-functions/secondary-functions/miscellaneous-functions/get_lock).

### Round-3 fixes verified

- **PostgreSQL indirect dispatch: fixed.** `catalog/postgres.rs:118–126` rejects post-initdb (`oid >= 16384`) non-trigger/event-trigger functions, casts, operators, conversions and domain constraints, regardless of schema. It rejects every RLS policy (stronger than an OID-only filter). Post-initdb views are checked by definition with a bounded count. Original custom cast/operator fixtures now encounter this gate even when their implementation is an unsafe built-in. Trigger-returning functions remain exempt because SELECT cannot invoke them as ordinary functions. Live test source includes both original reproductions; reviewer did not execute live fixtures.
- **Historical preview safety: fixed for newly generated agent previews.** `agentAnswerParts` sets `agent: true`; message parts preserve that flag through persistence/reload; `SqlPreviewPart` forwards it to the card. `handleRunSql` authenticates first, calls `aiAgentSqlSafety` with the exact SQL and the runtime connection used for the ensuing `runSqlQuery`, and stops on unknown/error. Mounted regressions cover authentication ordering, reopening on `conn-2`, newly unsafe catalog rejection and unchanged legacy behavior. Persisted labels are display metadata. No source path found that strips the flag from newly generated agent previews.

### Residuals and Phase 2 disposition

- **Separate validate/run calls:** remains a real catalog/connection consistency race; a concurrent change after successful validation can affect execution. Treat as a disclosed **Phase 2 limitation**, not another round-4 blocker: current phase adds user-clicked previews and fixed metadata tools, not autonomous data execution, and the requested Phase 3 R4 executor owns the single validate-and-execute contract. This review does not certify race-free read-only execution. The system-schema bypass above is independent of this residual and blocks Phase 2 now.
- **Unbounded tracker drain:** coordinator and safety check still wait for owned work to finish; timeout constants do not prove a wall-clock bound for stalled native/blocking work. No concrete permanently stuck production path identified in this re-review. Retaining session/slot ownership is safer than releasing it with live work. Keep as **Phase 2 limitation**; Phase 3 must establish native stop/liveness evidence before extending execution. Existing cooperative cleanup test proves ordering, not universal native bounds.
- Prior main-only SQLite metadata, disarm-before-unlock, cancellation ownership, metadata opt-out, context forwarding, fail-before-tool fallback, semantic truncation and authoritative bounded history fixes remain present. No additional concrete regressions found in those inspected paths. Legacy preview Run behavior intentionally remains unchanged; this verdict is scoped to agent previews, not a new safety guarantee for legacy SQL.

### Independent verification

- `npm run build`: passed; existing externalization/dynamic-import/chunk-size/plugin-timing warnings.
- `npm run test:run`: **122 files / 688 tests passed**.
- `cargo test --offline --locked ai::agent -- --skip http_`: **80 passed / 7 ignored / 207 filtered out**, both default and `--features app-store`.
- `cargo test --offline --locked commands::ai_agent`: **1 passed / 293 filtered out** (default).
- `cargo fmt --all -- --check` and `git diff --check`: passed before report append.
- Full Rust suite/clippy not rerun this round; previous socket-bind failures remain environment limits, not findings. Live DB identity/cancel tests, native UI/MAS behavior and real provider/database round trip not independently run. Docker access denied. No source files modified by harnesses.

Next: Claude closes the MySQL/MariaDB excluded-schema identity gap, adds targeted regressions and records verification for re-review. Phase 3 residuals remain explicitly deferred.

## Review: AI agent Phase 2 — round 5

**Final verdict: CHANGES REQUIRED.** Reviewer: Codex. Date: 2026-10-13. Branch: `feat/ai-agent`; scope: `git diff HEAD` plus untracked Phase 2 files. **1 P1; no additional P0/P2 findings.** Phase 1 verdict unchanged; Phase 3 not approved.

Read `AGENTS.md`, `/Users/lamtung/.codex/RTK.md`, `.agent/WORKFLOW.md`, all four prior Phase 2 reviews, and Claude's “Phase 2 — Review fixes, round 5” in `.agent/plans/ai-agent.md`. Rechecked backend safety/catalog/coordinator paths, frontend Run/routing, history, privacy controls and regression tests. Independent read-only safety pass also examined the remaining identity gate. Ignored `.claude/launch.json`. Only this report edited; no application source edits, staging or commit.

### Finding

1. **P1 — Reserved definer strings still let custom system-schema views bypass safety.** `src-tauri/src/ai/agent/catalog/mysql.rs:119–125,178–206`. The blanket `mysql`/`sys` exclusion is removed, but `not_server_owned` now skips any view or function in either schema whose `DEFINER` string is `mysql.sys@localhost` or `mariadb.sys@localhost`. That string does not prove the object is a stock server definition. An administrator able to name another definer can install this MySQL fixture (substitute `mariadb.sys` for MariaDB):

   ```sql
   CREATE DEFINER='mysql.sys'@'localhost' SQL SECURITY INVOKER
   VIEW mysql.agent_lock_view AS
   SELECT GET_LOCK('agent_round5_lock', 0) AS acquired;
   ```

   Grant a normal connected user SELECT on the view. In the otherwise passing baseline used by the live identity fixture, the new view adds no stored function and is excluded from the definition scan. Suggested `SELECT acquired FROM mysql.agent_lock_view` passes the exact Rust classifier for **both engines** and the exact frontend heuristic. Both answer-time safety and post-authentication Run revalidation therefore return `read_only`; `AiAssistantMessageCard.tsx:133–163` forwards it to unrestricted `runSqlQuery`, which evaluates the view and acquires a session lock. `SQL SECURITY INVOKER` makes execution independent of the reserved account's privileges or login restrictions. The same construction works in `sys`.

   This is a stable catalog bypass, not the deferred validate-then-run race. The administrator prerequisite was already present in round 4; the person clicking Run needs only the granted SELECT privilege. Round-5 evidence calls the definer exception “accepted”, but an implementer note does not establish human acceptance or purity of arbitrary objects carrying that definer.

   **Fix:** remove trust based solely on schema/definer. Check reachable view definitions and fail closed for missing/unsafe definitions; any compatibility exception for stock objects must establish their actual definitions and transitive dispatch rather than owner strings. Add reserved-definer `SQL SECURITY INVOKER` unsafe-view fixtures in `mysql` and `sys` for both engines, through backend eligibility and mounted Run revalidation. The current live test at `catalog/mysql.rs:562–601` creates views with the default definer and misses this branch.

   **Evidence boundary:** source predicate and Run path verified; temporary `/tmp` harness compiled the exact Rust classifier with serialization-only annotations removed, yielding `ReadOnly` for mysql/mariadb; Node harness transpiled the exact frontend `isReadOnlySql`, yielding `true`. Live view evaluation is inferred from primary server documentation, not independently executed. MySQL permits naming another definer with the applicable administrative privileges and explains that INVOKER ignores the definer for execution: [stored object security](https://dev.mysql.com/doc/refman/8.4/en/stored-objects-security.html). MariaDB documents arbitrary definers with SET USER and invoker execution: [CREATE VIEW](https://mariadb.com/docs/server/server-usage/views/create-view). The lock persists beyond transaction completion: [MariaDB GET_LOCK](https://mariadb.com/docs/server/reference/sql-functions/secondary-functions/miscellaneous-functions/get_lock).

### Earlier fixes and round-4 disposition

- **Round-4 P1: partially fixed, not closed.** Ordinary custom views/functions in `mysql` and `sys` are now inspected, and the expanded ignored live fixture covers them. Reserved-definer objects remain unchecked as described above.
- **PostgreSQL indirect dispatch remains fixed.** Gate rejects post-initdb non-trigger functions, casts, operators, conversions and domain constraints regardless of schema; rejects RLS policies; checks bounded post-initdb view definitions. Original overload/cast/operator paths remain blocked.
- **Execution-time Run check remains fixed.** Newly generated agent previews retain `agent: true` through parts/persistence; Run authenticates first, then checks the exact SQL against the runtime connection used for execution. Unknown/error stops execution. Mounted changed-connection/catalog and authentication-order regressions pass. Legacy behavior remains unchanged.
- **Cancellation/session ownership remains fixed in inspected paths.** Tracker-owned operations retain pooled sessions through interruption/cleanup; checkout is bounded and cancellation-aware; MySQL kill uses an independent connection; interrupted targets are discarded. SQLite arming rechecks cancellation/deadline, and `Held::drop` disarms before releasing the shared connection, including unwind.
- **Other earlier fixes remain present.** SQLite metadata pragmas explicitly target main; bounded/idempotent history fitting updates visible state before storage; bounded visible conversation/context forwarding preserves SQL-chip filtering; fallback is limited to the pre-tool terminal path; column-details opt-out strips defaults/comments before serialization; provider/plaintext-history disclosures remain; semantic truncation reaches persisted step summaries. No additional concrete regression found in these inspected paths.
- **Metadata trust boundary remains intact.** Only three fixed, parameter-bound metadata tools are registered; no model SQL or application-row tool enters their executors. Arguments are validated; executor errors are fixed safe reasons; step records omit arguments/results/provider replay. No new sensitive logging/analytics path found.

### Independent verification and limitations

- `npm run build`: **passed**, existing externalization/dynamic-import/chunk-size/plugin-timing warnings.
- `npm run test:run`: **122 files / 688 tests passed**.
- `cargo test --offline --locked ai::agent -- --skip http_`: **80 passed / 7 ignored / 207 filtered out**, both default and `--features app-store`.
- `cargo test --offline --locked commands::ai_agent`: **1 passed / 293 filtered out** (default).
- `cargo fmt --all -- --check` and `git diff --check`: passed before report append.
- Full Rust suite/clippy not repeated; prior socket-bind failures remain environment limits. `docker ps` again failed with socket permission denied; ignored live identity/cancel fixtures were inspected, not independently run. Claude's reported live runs remain implementer evidence. No native UI/MAS or real provider/database round trip performed.
- The documented **validate-then-run gap, unbounded tracker wait, shared SQLite connection and absent live provider round trip** remain Phase 2 limitations for the Phase 3 R4 executor; no new concrete blocking path established for those residuals. They do not explain or cover the stable definer bypass above.

Next: Claude closes reserved-definer trust, adds the targeted regressions and records verification for re-review. No source changes authorized by this report.

## Review: AI agent Phase 2 — round 6

**Final verdict: CHANGES REQUIRED.** Reviewer: Codex. Date: 2026-10-13. Branch: `feat/ai-agent`; scope: `git diff HEAD` plus untracked Phase 2 files. **1 P1; no additional P0/P2 findings.** Round-5 reserved-definer P1 is fixed in code; removing the stored-function check introduces a separate stable bypass. Phase 1 verdict unchanged; Phase 3 not approved.

Read `AGENTS.md`, `/Users/lamtung/.codex/RTK.md`, `.agent/WORKFLOW.md`, all five earlier Phase 2 reviews, and Claude's “Phase 2 — Review fixes, round 6” in `.agent/plans/ai-agent.md`. Rechecked safety/catalog/coordinator code, frontend routing and Run, privacy/history controls and regression tests. Ignored `.claude/launch.json`. Only this report edited; no application source edits, staging or commit.

### Finding

1. **P1 — Cross-engine function allow-list defeats the built-in precedence premise after the stored-function check is removed.** `src-tauri/src/ai/agent/catalog/mysql.rs:167–191`; `src-tauri/src/ai/agent/sql_safety.rs:288–455,522–539` (notably `PRINTF` at 377 and `DATE_TRUNC` at 400). Built-in precedence protects `lower`, but the shared allow-list also trusts PostgreSQL/SQLite names that are not native functions on MySQL/MariaDB. A nonconflicting stored function can be called **without** a schema qualifier in the current database. In the otherwise passing ordinary-account baseline, install this fixture in that connection's current database and grant EXECUTE to the connected account:

   ```sql
   CREATE FUNCTION date_trunc(x INT) RETURNS INT
   DETERMINISTIC RETURN GET_LOCK('agent_round6_lock', 0);
   ```

   Suggested `SELECT date_trunc(1)` returns **ReadOnly** from the exact Rust classifier for both `mysql` and `mariadb`, and **true** from the exact frontend heuristic. It contains no qualified call, view or denied keyword. The new `only_builtin_code` inspects only views; adding this function does not change a passing view scan. Both answer-time classification and post-authentication Run revalidation therefore accept it, and `AiAssistantMessageCard.tsx:133–163` forwards it to unrestricted `runSqlQuery`. Function evaluation acquires a session lock. The misleading DETERMINISTIC declaration does not enforce purity. This is a stable catalog path, independent of the deferred validate-then-run race; no concurrent DDL, stale label, forged IPC or reserved definer is required.

   **Fix:** do not remove routine identity protection while trusting a union of function names across engines. Restrict accepted calls to positively established native functions for the actual engine/server version and SQL mode, or fail closed when non-native stored/loadable dispatch can resolve. Audit all function-like paths, including keyword/type exceptions, rather than patching only these two names. Preserve ordinary built-in reporting and the all-schema view checks. Add a non-built-in but currently allow-listed stored-function fixture (e.g. `date_trunc`) for both servers through backend safety and mounted Run eligibility; verify no lock is acquired. The existing round-6 `lower` fixture tests only the collision case and cannot establish this broader claim.

   **Evidence boundary:** a temporary `/tmp` harness compiled the exact current Rust classifier with serialization-only annotations removed; `SELECT date_trunc(1)` and `SELECT printf(1)` returned ReadOnly for both engines, whereas `SELECT agent_t.date_trunc(1)` returned Unknown. An extracted exact frontend `isReadOnlySql` returned true for both unqualified examples. Catalog and Run paths verified statically. Live stored-function execution was **not independently run** because Docker socket access is denied. Dispatch/lock execution is inferred from primary server documentation, not presented as a live reproduction: [MySQL function resolution](https://dev.mysql.com/doc/refman/8.4/en/function-resolution.html) explicitly distinguishes names with a native implementation from names resolving to stored/loadable functions; its [built-in reference](https://dev.mysql.com/doc/refman/8.4/en/built-in-function-reference.html) has neither DATE_TRUNC nor PRINTF. [MariaDB CREATE FUNCTION](https://mariadb.com/docs/server/reference/sql-statements/data-definition/create/create-function) permits ordinary calls and requires qualification specifically for built-in-name collisions; [MariaDB date/time functions](https://mariadb.com/docs/server/reference/sql-functions/date-time-functions) does not list DATE_TRUNC. [GET_LOCK](https://mariadb.com/docs/server/reference/sql-functions/secondary-functions/miscellaneous-functions/get_lock) has session effects independent of transaction completion.

### Round-5 fix and earlier guarantees

- **Reserved-definer views: fixed in code.** `not_server_owned` now excludes only `information_schema` and `performance_schema`; no `DEFINER` predicate remains. Visible views in both `mysql` and `sys` are scanned regardless of owner. Empty/missing definitions and more than 200 definitions fail closed; GET_LOCK definitions classify unknown. The ignored live fixture now creates reserved-definer SQL SECURITY INVOKER views in both schemas and grants SELECT/SHOW VIEW to the ordinary account. Implementer's reported MySQL 8.0.46/MariaDB 11.8.9 runs remain implementer evidence, not independent verification.
- **Stored-function removal: not safe as implemented.** Built-in-name precedence plus qualified-call rejection is valid for actual native-name collisions such as `lower`; it does not cover the cross-engine allow-list. A blanket stored-function rejection may be too restrictive for stock sys routines, but usability does not establish the identity of accepted non-native names.
- **PostgreSQL indirect dispatch remains blocked.** Catalog gate still rejects post-initdb non-trigger functions, casts, operators, conversions and domain constraints regardless of schema, all RLS policies, and unsafe/missing/over-cap view definitions. Original overload/cast/operator regressions retain their protection.
- **Execution-time Run revalidation remains present.** Agent previews retain `agent: true` through parts and persistence. Run checks exact SQL on the runtime connection after authentication; unknown/error stops execution. Mounted connection/catalog-change and ordering tests pass. This recheck cannot close the current classifier/catalog bypass. Legacy preview behavior remains unchanged.
- **Cancellation and SQLite fixes remain present.** Tracker-owned operations retain target sessions through interruption/cleanup; acquisition is bounded and cancellation-aware; MySQL kill uses an independent connection; interrupted targets are discarded. SQLite checks cancellation/deadline while arming, disarms before unlocking (including unwind), and explicitly qualifies metadata pragmas with main. Focused regressions pass.
- **Other earlier fixes remain present.** Bounded visible history/context forwarding preserves SQL-chip filtering; fitted history updates authoritative visible state before persistence; oversized/legacy metadata regressions pass. Fallback remains pre-tool only. Column-details opt-out strips defaults/comments before encoding; provider/plaintext-history disclosures remain. Semantic truncation reaches step summaries. Only the three fixed, parameter-bound metadata tools are registered; no model SQL/application-row execution enters them. Safe errors and summary-only steps preserve the reviewed privacy boundary. No further concrete regression found in these inspected paths.

### Independent verification and limitations

- `npm run build`: **passed**, existing externalization/dynamic-import/chunk-size/plugin-timing warnings.
- `npm run test:run`: **122 files / 688 tests passed**.
- `cargo test --offline --locked ai::agent -- --skip http_`: **80 passed / 7 ignored / 207 filtered out**, both default and `--features app-store`.
- `cargo test --offline --locked commands::ai_agent`: **1 passed / 293 filtered out** (default).
- `cargo fmt --all -- --check` and `git diff --check`: **passed** before report append.
- Full Rust suite/clippy not repeated this round; prior socket-bind failures remain environment limits. `docker ps` failed with socket permission denied; ignored live identity/cancellation fixtures inspected but not independently executed. No native UI/MAS or real provider/database round trip performed. Green tests do not cover the non-native stored-function fixture above.
- Documented **validate-then-run gap, unbounded tracker wait, shared SQLite connection and absent live provider round trip** remain Phase 2 limitations within Phase 3 R4 executor scope. No new blocking path established for those residuals; they do not cover the stable routine-dispatch bypass above.

Next: Claude closes non-native routine dispatch, adds targeted regressions and records verification for re-review. No source changes authorized by this report.

## Review: AI agent Phase 2 — round 7

**Final verdict: CHANGES REQUIRED.** Reviewer: Codex. Date: 2026-10-13. Branch: `feat/ai-agent`; scope: `git diff HEAD` plus untracked Phase 2 files. **1 P1; no additional P0/P2 findings.** The round-6 `date_trunc`/`printf` allow-list bypass is fixed, but the same routine-dispatch protection is still bypassable through keyword exemptions. Phase 1 verdict unchanged; Phase 3 not approved.

Read `AGENTS.md`, `/Users/lamtung/.codex/RTK.md`, `.agent/WORKFLOW.md`, all six earlier Phase 2 reviews and Claude's “Phase 2 — Review fixes, round 7” in `.agent/plans/ai-agent.md`. Rechecked safety/catalog/coordinator paths, actual frontend Run eligibility and execution, history/context, metadata privacy and focused regressions. Ignored `.claude/launch.json`. Only this report edited; no source edits, staging or commit.

### Finding

1. **P1 — Keyword exemptions bypass both native-name validation and qualified-call rejection.** `src-tauri/src/ai/agent/sql_safety.rs:657–658` skips any word in `PAREN_KEYWORDS` followed by `(` **before** checking its preceding token or the dialect's function list at `:660–676`. Consequently `SELECT agent_t.filter(1)` and `SELECT agent_t.within(1)` return **ReadOnly** for both `mysql` and `mariadb`. These are schema-qualified stored-function calls, not aggregate FILTER clauses or other keyword grammar. Neither FILTER nor WITHIN is in `MYSQL_FUNCTIONS`, so auditing that list does not cover this path.

   In the otherwise passing ordinary-account baseline used by `agent_mysql_live_identity`, install this fixture as an account allowed to create routines; the connected account needs EXECUTE on it:

   ```sql
   CREATE FUNCTION agent_t.`filter`(x INT) RETURNS INT
   DETERMINISTIC RETURN GET_LOCK('agent_round7_lock', 0);
   ```

   Suggested `SELECT agent_t.filter(1)` passes the exact Rust classifier and exact frontend `isReadOnlySql`. Adding the routine adds no view, so `catalog/mysql.rs:174–191` leaves `only_builtin_code` true in that baseline: it scans only view definitions. Both answer-time safety and post-authentication Run revalidation use this same syntax/catalog pair (`commands/ai_agent.rs:184–200`); the preview gets Run, and `AiAssistantMessageCard.tsx:147–163` forwards the query to unrestricted `runSqlQuery`. The stored function acquires a session lock. No concurrent DDL, stale label, forged IPC or reserved definer is required. This is a stable Phase 2 eligibility defect, outside the deferred validate-then-run gap.

   **Fix:** reject qualified function calls before keyword/type/alias exemptions, and recognize keyword exceptions only in valid dialect-specific grammatical positions. Merely moving qualification earlier leaves unqualified `SELECT filter(1)` accepted on MySQL/MariaDB; it also returns ReadOnly in the current classifier. Audit every exemption, including type/alias handling, rather than extending only `MYSQL_FUNCTIONS`. Add qualified and unqualified non-native keyword-named routine regressions through backend eligibility and mounted Run, plus live fixtures for both servers verifying no lock is acquired. Preserve ordinary IN/EXISTS, CTE/alias, CAST and PostgreSQL aggregate FILTER reporting.

   **Evidence boundary:** a temporary `/tmp` harness compiled the exact current Rust classifier with serde-only annotations removed. Both qualified examples and unqualified `SELECT filter(1)` returned ReadOnly for both engines; original `SELECT date_trunc(1)` and `SELECT printf(1)` returned Unknown. A Node harness extracted the exact frontend heuristic; all three keyword-call examples returned true. Catalog and Run paths inspected statically. Live stored-function evaluation was **not independently run**: Docker socket access is denied. Server dispatch and locking effects are inferred from primary documentation: [MySQL function resolution](https://dev.mysql.com/doc/refman/8.4/en/function-resolution.html), [MySQL qualified identifiers](https://dev.mysql.com/doc/refman/8.4/en/keywords.html) (words after a period are identifiers, including reserved words), [MariaDB CREATE FUNCTION](https://mariadb.com/docs/server/reference/sql-statements/data-definition/create/create-function) and [GET_LOCK](https://mariadb.com/docs/server/reference/sql-functions/secondary-functions/miscellaneous-functions/get_lock). The round-7 ignored live test iterates only `MYSQL_FUNCTIONS`, not the keyword exemptions, and never exercises this stored-function path through Run.

### Round-6 fix and earlier guarantees

- **Non-native names in the function list: fixed in code.** MySQL/MariaDB now use their own native-name list; PostgreSQL/SQLite retain their list. The original date_trunc/printf bypass is independently rejected; unit regressions also reject to_char/strftime on MySQL/MariaDB. Ordinary native lower/count/date reporting retains coverage. Routine dispatch as a whole remains open because of the P1 above. Claude's reported native-name checks on MySQL 8.0.46/MariaDB 11.8.9 remain implementer evidence.
- **Earlier MySQL view fixes remain present.** Only information_schema/performance_schema are excluded; mysql/sys views are checked regardless of definer. Missing, unsafe and over-cap definitions fail closed. No schema/definer trust exception has returned.
- **PostgreSQL indirect dispatch protection remains present.** Catalog checks reject post-initdb non-trigger functions, casts, operators, conversions and domain constraints across schemas, all RLS policies, and missing/unsafe/over-cap view definitions. Original overload/cast/operator paths remain blocked in the inspected code.
- **Execution-time revalidation remains present.** Agent previews retain `agent: true` in parts/persistence. Run authenticates first and checks exact SQL on the execution runtime connection; unknown/error stops execution. Mounted changed-connection/catalog and authentication-order tests pass. Revalidation repeats, and therefore cannot stop, the new classifier bypass. Legacy behavior remains unchanged.
- **Cancellation and SQLite fixes remain present.** Tracker-owned tasks retain pooled targets through interruption/cleanup; checkout is bounded and cancellation-aware; MySQL cancellation uses an independent connection and interrupted targets are discarded. SQLite arming rechecks cancel/deadline, disarms before unlocking including unwind, and metadata pragmas explicitly select main. Focused tests pass.
- **Context, persistence and privacy fixes remain present.** Bounded visible history/context forwarding preserves SQL-chip filtering; history fitting updates visible state before persistence, with oversized/legacy/idempotence tests passing. Fallback remains pre-tool only in the inspected production flow. Column-details opt-out strips defaults/comments before serialization; provider/plaintext-history disclosures remain. Semantic truncation reaches step summaries. Only three fixed, parameter-bound metadata tools are registered; no model SQL/application-row tool enters the executors. Safe errors and summary-only steps retain the privacy boundary. No additional concrete regression found in these inspected paths.

### Independent verification and limitations

- `npm run build`: **passed**, existing externalization/dynamic-import/chunk-size/plugin-timing warnings.
- `npm run test:run`: **122 files / 688 tests passed**.
- `cargo test --offline --locked ai::agent -- --skip http_`: **80 passed / 7 ignored / 207 filtered out**, default and `--features app-store`.
- `cargo test --offline --locked commands::ai_agent`: **1 passed / 293 filtered out**, default.
- `cargo fmt --all -- --check` and `git diff --check`: **passed** before report append.
- Full Rust suite/clippy not repeated; prior socket-bind failures remain environment limits. `docker ps` failed with socket permission denied. Ignored live identity/cancel fixtures inspected, not independently executed. No native UI/MAS inspection or live provider/database round trip performed. Green tests do not cover the keyword-named stored-function bypass.
- Documented **validate-then-run gap, unbounded tracker wait, shared SQLite connection and absent live provider round trip** remain Phase 2 limitations within Phase 3 R4 executor scope. No new blocking path established for those residuals; they do not cover the stable P1 above.

Next: Claude closes keyword-exemption routine dispatch, adds targeted regressions and records verification for re-review. No source changes authorized by this report.

## Review: AI agent Phase 2 — round 8

**Final verdict: CHANGES REQUIRED.** Reviewer: Codex. Date: 2026-10-13. Branch: `feat/ai-agent`; scope: `git diff HEAD` plus untracked Phase 2 files. **1 P1; no additional P0/P2 findings.** Round-7 keyword/qualified-word bypass is fixed in code; literal-token handling still bypasses routine validation. Phase 1 verdict unchanged; Phase 3 not approved.

Read `AGENTS.md`, `/Users/lamtung/.codex/RTK.md`, `.agent/WORKFLOW.md`, all seven previous Phase 2 reviews and Claude's “Phase 2 — Review fixes, round 8” in `.agent/plans/ai-agent.md`. Rechecked backend syntax/catalog/coordinator paths, actual frontend preview/Run routing, history/context, metadata privacy and regression coverage. Ignored `.claude/launch.json`. Only this report edited; no application source edits, staging or commit.

### Finding

1. **P1 — MySQL/MariaDB identifiers tokenized as literals bypass every function-call check.** `src-tauri/src/ai/agent/sql_safety.rs:127–131,659–701` (also double-quote handling at `:105–122`); `catalog/mysql.rs:174–191`. The numeric branch consumes any ASCII alphanumeric run, including a valid digit-leading identifier, and labels it `Literal`. `classify` validates calls only after `Word` and `Quoted`; `Literal` falls through. Independent execution of the exact current classifier returns **ReadOnly** for both `SELECT 1agent(1)` and `SELECT agent_t.1agent(1)` on both engines. The exact frontend `isReadOnlySql` returns **true** for both. In the otherwise passing ordinary-account baseline, install this fixture in the connection's current database:

   ```sql
   CREATE FUNCTION agent_t.`1agent`(x INT) RETURNS INT
   DETERMINISTIC RETURN GET_LOCK('agent_round8_lock', 0);
   GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION agent_t.`1agent` TO 'agent_ro'@'%';
   ```

   Both servers permit digit-leading unquoted identifiers containing letters; this name deliberately avoids ambiguous exponent notation. Suggested `SELECT 1agent(1)` resolves to the stored function, and the qualified variant also escapes the new preceding-dot rejection because its name is not a `Word`. Adding the routine adds no view, so `only_builtin_code` remains true in that baseline. Answer-time `sqlSafety` and post-authentication `aiAgentSqlSafety` therefore both accept the query; the preview offers Run and `AiAssistantMessageCard.tsx:147–163` sends it through unrestricted `runSqlQuery`. Its function body acquires a session lock. No concurrent DDL, historical label, forged IPC or reserved definer is needed.

   **Same unchecked-token path under ANSI_QUOTES:** MySQL/MariaDB double-quoted text is always tokenized as `Literal`, although that SQL mode makes it an identifier. The exact classifier also accepts `SELECT "agent_lock"(1)` and `SELECT agent_t."agent_lock"(1)` for both engines, and the frontend accepts both. With an existing stored function `agent_t.agent_lock(x)` whose body takes a lock, and ANSI_QUOTES enabled for execution sessions, these are another route to user code. The safety command neither checks SQL mode nor rejects literal-looking call names. The digit-leading reproduction requires no special SQL mode.

   **Fix:** distinguish numeric literals from the full identifier grammar, and fail closed on ambiguous SQL-mode quoting. Apply call/qualification checks to every token that can denote a callable identifier; a literal token followed by `(` must not silently pass. Audit numeric, quoted, Unicode and qualifier forms rather than patching one spelling. Add both engines' backend eligibility and mounted Run regressions for digit-leading unqualified/qualified routines and ANSI_QUOTES quoted routines, with live fixtures verifying no lock is acquired. Preserve ordinary numeric literals, native aggregates, IN/EXISTS, CTE aliases and CAST reporting.

   **Evidence boundary:** `/tmp` Rust harness compiled the exact current classifier with only serde serialization annotations removed; Node harness transpiled the exact frontend heuristic. All four new examples returned ReadOnly/true for both engine keys. Catalog and Run routing verified statically. Live function execution was **not independently run**: `docker ps` failed with Docker socket permission denied. Server execution is an inference from documented identifier/function dispatch and lock semantics, not claimed as a live reproduction. Primary references: [MySQL identifier grammar and ANSI_QUOTES](https://dev.mysql.com/doc/refman/8.4/en/identifiers.html), [MariaDB identifier names](https://mariadb.com/docs/server/reference/sql-structure/sql-language-structure/identifier-names), [MySQL function resolution](https://dev.mysql.com/doc/refman/8.4/en/function-resolution.html), [MariaDB CREATE FUNCTION](https://mariadb.com/docs/server/reference/sql-statements/data-definition/create/create-function), [GET_LOCK](https://mariadb.com/docs/server/reference/sql-functions/secondary-functions/miscellaneous-functions/get_lock).

### Round-7 fix and earlier guarantees

- **Keyword/qualified-word finding: fixed in code.** Preceding-dot rejection now runs before keyword/type/alias exemptions. Independent harness rejects `agent_t.filter(1)`, unqualified `filter(1)` and `within(1), rows(1)` for MySQL/MariaDB. New Rust regressions cover qualified keywords across other dialects. MySQL/MariaDB have a separate reserved-word list; the ignored live fixture attempts unquoted routine creation for each entry. Claude's reported server runs remain implementer evidence. Native COUNT/IN/CAST examples still classify ReadOnly. This fix does not protect callable identifiers represented as Literal.
- **Earlier MySQL view/native-name fixes remain present.** Only information_schema/performance_schema are excluded; mysql/sys definitions are checked regardless of definer. Missing, unsafe and over-cap definitions fail closed. Separate MYSQL_FUNCTIONS rejects original date_trunc/printf/to_char/strftime examples. No schema/definer exception has returned.
- **PostgreSQL identity checks remain present.** Post-initdb non-trigger functions, casts, operators, conversions and domain constraints across schemas, all RLS policies, and missing/unsafe/over-cap views still prevent trust. Original overload/cast/operator fixtures remain covered by the inspected gate.
- **Run revalidation remains present.** Agent previews retain `agent: true` through parts and persistence. Run authenticates first, then validates exact SQL on the runtime connection used for execution; unknown/error stops it. Changed-connection/catalog and authentication-order mounted tests pass. Revalidation repeats the new lexical bypass; legacy preview behavior remains unchanged.
- **Cancellation and SQLite fixes remain present.** Tracker-owned tasks retain pooled sessions through interruption/cleanup; acquisition is bounded/cancellation-aware, MySQL kill uses an independent connection, and interrupted targets are discarded. SQLite arming rechecks cancellation/deadline, disarms before unlock including unwind, and metadata pragmas explicitly select main. Focused regressions pass.
- **Context/history/privacy fixes remain present.** Bounded visible conversation/context forwarding retains SQL-chip filtering; exact-envelope history fitting updates visible state before persistence and retains oversized/legacy/idempotence coverage. Fallback remains pre-tool only in the inspected production path. Column-details opt-out strips defaults/comments before serialization; provider and plaintext-history disclosures remain. Semantic truncation reaches step summaries. Only three fixed, parameter-bound metadata tools are registered; no model-written SQL or application-row tool enters these executors. Safe errors and summary-only steps preserve the reviewed privacy boundary. No additional concrete regression found in these inspected paths.

### Independent verification and limitations

- `npm run test:run`: **122 files / 688 tests passed**.
- `cargo test --offline --locked ai::agent -- --skip http_`: **80 passed / 7 ignored / 207 filtered out**, default and `--features app-store`.

Docker access denied; ignored live identity/cancellation fixtures inspected, not independently executed. No native UI/MAS or real provider/database round trip performed. Existing green tests do not cover literal-token callable identifiers.

## Review: AI agent Phase 2 — round 9

**Final verdict: CHANGES REQUIRED.** Reviewer: Codex. Date: 2026-10-13. Branch: `feat/ai-agent`; scope: `git diff HEAD` plus untracked Phase 2 files. **1 P1; no additional P0/P2 findings.** Round-8 identifier/literal finding is fixed in code. A separate PostgreSQL comment-termination bypass still enables Run for a side-effecting query. Phase 1 verdict unchanged; Phase 3 not approved.

Read `AGENTS.md`, `/Users/lamtung/.codex/RTK.md`, `.agent/WORKFLOW.md`, all eight previous Phase 2 review sections and Claude's “Phase 2 — Review fixes, round 9” in `.agent/plans/ai-agent.md`. Rechecked SQL syntax/catalog gates, frontend answer-time eligibility and execution-time Run, catalog ownership/cancellation, SQLite scope, history/context and privacy paths. Ignored `.claude/launch.json`. Only this report edited; no application source edits, staging or commit.

### Finding

1. **P1 — PostgreSQL carriage returns end server comments but do not end classifier comments.** `src-tauri/src/ai/agent/sql_safety.rs:70–72` scans a `--` comment until LF only. PostgreSQL's scanner ends that comment at either LF or CR. This exact SQL, with a literal U+000D at `\r`, returns **ReadOnly** from the current Rust classifier and **true** from the exact frontend `isReadOnlySql`:

   ```text
   SELECT 1 -- note\r, pg_advisory_lock(1)
   ```

   PostgreSQL sees one valid SELECT with two expressions; `pg_advisory_lock(1)` is outside the comment and acquires a session-level lock. The classifier sees only `SELECT 1`. A clean initdb database with no disqualifying custom dispatch objects or views passes `PgCatalog::only_builtin_code` (`catalog/postgres.rs:112–153`): the called function is a stock built-in, so the catalog gate cannot compensate for losing its tokens. Answer-time `sqlSafety` (`src/lib/ai-assistant/agent.ts:186–194`) advertises read_only/Run, and post-authentication Run revalidation repeats the same faulty check (`commands/ai_agent.rs:185–199`). `AiAssistantMessageCard.tsx:147–163` then sends the unchanged SQL to `runSqlQuery`; its operation DTO (`src/lib/tauri/query.ts:298–307`) has no read_only flag, and PostgreSQL prepares/executes that SELECT. Even transaction rollback would not release a session advisory lock. No stored routine, catalog mutation, stale label, SQL mode, multi-statement support or validate/run race is required. This is a stable Phase 2 eligibility defect.

   **Fix:** model PostgreSQL's CR and LF comment terminators, or reject ambiguous line-ending syntax before classification. Audit line comments per supported dialect; do not normalize execution SQL differently from validation SQL. Add CR-only, LF and CRLF regressions, including a forbidden built-in after CR, through backend eligibility and mounted agent Run. Preserve ordinary trailing comments and retain Insert for unknown SQL.

   **Evidence boundary:** a temporary `/tmp` harness compiled the exact current Rust classifier with only serde serialization annotations removed; the query above returned ReadOnly. A Node harness transpiled/executed the exact frontend heuristic; it returned true. Catalog and unchanged-SQL execution routing inspected statically. Server parsing and lock acquisition are inferred from primary sources, **not claimed as a live database reproduction**: PostgreSQL's [scanner source](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/postgres/postgres/REL_18_STABLE/src/backend/parser/scan.l) explicitly defines `newline [\n\r]`, `non_newline [^\n\r]` and `comment ("--"{non_newline}*)` (lines 175–199); its [advisory-lock documentation](https://www.postgresql.org/docs/18/functions-admin.html#FUNCTIONS-ADVISORY-LOCKS) documents session-level lock acquisition/release. Docker socket permission denied prevents independent live execution.

### Round-8 fix and earlier guarantees

- **Callable identifier/literal cases: fixed.** Identifier runs now include digit-leading names and MySQL `$`; a run becomes Literal only when wholly numeric. Literal and Quoted tokens immediately before `(` fail closed. Non-ASCII text outside strings/comments is rejected; ASCII-only whitespace prevents U+00A0 from splitting a callable identifier. Independent exact-classifier harness rejects `1agent(1)`, `agent_t.1agent(1)`, `"agent_lock"(1)` and `lower<U+00A0>(1)` on both MySQL and MariaDB (also PostgreSQL/SQLite). Rust regressions additionally cover qualified ANSI_QUOTES names, `$lower`, the non-ASCII comment-prefix case and numeric-looking calls. Ordinary numbers, digit-leading table references and non-ASCII string literals retain positive coverage. The expanded ignored live fixture creates/calls `1agent` and checks its lock; Claude's reported server runs remain implementer evidence. No new mounted/live ANSI_QUOTES-specific round-trip coverage was added, but the token-level rejection is unconditional and the existing unknown/error Run gate remains intact.
- **Earlier SQL fixes remain present.** Executable comments fail closed; qualified-call rejection precedes keyword/type exemptions; MySQL/MariaDB retain separate native-function and reserved-keyword lists. Earlier date_trunc/printf and keyword routine cases remain covered. MySQL/sys views are checked regardless of definer; only information_schema/performance_schema are excluded, and missing/unsafe/over-cap definitions fail closed. PostgreSQL still rejects post-initdb non-trigger functions, casts, operators, conversions, domain constraints and all RLS policies, and checks bounded view definitions. No earlier schema/definer trust exception returned.
- **Run/context/history fixes remain present.** New agent previews retain `agent: true` through parts/persistence; authentication precedes exact-SQL/current-runtime revalidation; unknown/error stops execution. Bounded visible conversation/context forwarding retains SQL-chip filtering and excludes result rows/provider replay. Exact-envelope fitting updates visible history before storage; oversized-message, legacy metadata, idempotence and eviction regressions pass. Legacy fallback remains pre-tool in the inspected production flow; legacy preview behavior remains unchanged.
- **Catalog lifecycle/privacy fixes remain present.** Tracker-owned tasks retain pooled targets through interruption/cleanup; checkout is bounded/cancellation-aware; MySQL cancellation uses an independent connection and interrupted targets are discarded. SQLite arming rechecks cancellation/deadline, disarms before unlock including unwind, and all table/index metadata pragmas explicitly select main. Only three fixed, parameter-bound metadata tools are registered; model SQL/application-row tools are absent. Column-details opt-out strips comments/defaults before encoding; provider/plaintext-history disclosures remain. Semantic truncation reaches summary-only step records; safe executor errors retain the privacy boundary. No additional concrete regression found in these inspected paths.

### Independent verification and limitations

- `npm run build`: **passed**, existing externalization/dynamic-import/chunk-size/plugin-timing warnings.
- `npm run test:run`: **122 files / 688 tests passed**.
- `cargo test --offline --locked ai::agent -- --skip http_`: **80 passed / 7 ignored / 207 filtered out**, default and `--features app-store`.
- `cargo test --offline --locked commands::ai_agent`: **1 passed / 293 filtered out**, default.
- `cargo fmt --all -- --check` and `git diff --check`: **passed** before report append.
- Full Rust suite/clippy not repeated; previous socket-bind failures remain environment limits. `docker ps` failed with Docker socket permission denied. Ignored live identity/cancel fixtures inspected, not independently executed. No native UI/MAS or live provider/database round trip performed. Green tests do not cover CR-only PostgreSQL comment termination.
- Documented **validate-then-run gap, unbounded tracker wait, shared SQLite connection and absent live provider round trip** remain Phase 2 limitations within Phase 3 R4 executor scope. No new blocking path established for those residuals; they do not cover the stable lexical bypass above.
